HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1b6987821d47a3fadce8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:17:2\",\"root_001520\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bakışın gözetmeye dönüşme ihtimali, ileride denetimin reddedilmesiyle sınırlandırılır: Dikkatli gözlem ve hatırlatma meşrudur, fakat bunlar insanlar üzerinde egemenlik vermez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_000434/B003","root_001520/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_20ed07cb8996e46cf78a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:17:2\",\"root_001520\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:17:6\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bakmak, devenin görünür biçiminden onun ardındaki ölçü ve oran kurma işine geri giden bir inceleme yöntemidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_000434/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_28f48fce89c76cca4c9b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:17:6\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yaratma sözünün uydurma anlatıyı da çağrıştırması, görülen ölçülü biçimi sahte anlatının karşısına koyar; inceleme, kurulmuş gerçek ile örtülen ya da uydurulan iddia arasındaki farkı açığa çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2c926e767f92833b381d","text":"88:18-20 confirmed that the camel begins a deliberately parallel construction set rather than an isolated zoological example. 88:21-22 supplied a corrective surprise: observation leads to reminder, but the denial of control prevents `ن ظ ر` B009's watcher role from becoming supervisory domination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B002","root_001520/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_330d3eef7c69f0319558","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:17:2\",\"root_001520\",[\"B006\"]],[\"88:17:6\",\"root_000434\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bakışın karşılaştırmaya da açılması ve tekrarlanan soru kalıbı, deveyi canlı, gök, dağ ve yer arasında kurulan işlevsel benzerlikler dizisinin ilk örneği yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B001","root_000434/B002","root_001520/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3b8230cf672df9cb7f2a","text":"1. **Looking as reflective inspection of a pastoral body.** Evidence: `ن ظ ر` B001 directing sight/insight; `ء ب ل` B001 camels and tending; `خ ل ق` B002 originating, B003 complete well-proportioned form. `ك ي ف` is listed in missing branch inventories, so no branch ID is supplied for it. Structural cue: after garden furnishings, the discourse turns outward with repeated `إلى ... كيف`. Role: the camel is a visible, tended body whose creation is to be inspected. Abductive move: the signs chosen are not abstractions; they are ordinary ecological supports that disclose proportion. Reading change: the ayah reads as disciplined observation of fitted creaturely form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_82fa1cc8a970d0336b34","text":"This ayah became a hinge for the whole surah. It reactivated camel-hump, fatness, milk, water, pasture, and even the earlier humbled faces. The missing inventory for `ك ي ف` remained a constraint, but the repeated interrogative still functioned structurally as an inspection frame.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bd5e4fac821ecddff147","text":"4. **Observation is explicitly separated from supervisory control.** **Lexical:** `ن ظ ر` B009, a watcher who looks and guards, activated by contrast with `س ط ر` B003, overseeing control, negated in 88:22. **Cue:** the gaze-command leads through the reminder of 88:21 to `لَّسْتَ عَلَيْهِم بِمُصَيْطِرٍ`. **Role:** looking and reminding remain legitimate; converting the watcher into a controller is blocked. **Abduction:** I infer a role boundary across the sequence. **Reading change:** `ينظرون` inaugurates accountable attention without granting human domination over what or whom is observed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bf489b88d1d4cc28fd90","text":"3. **The camel is the first member of an analogical construction set.** **Lexical:** `ن ظ ر` B006, \"counterpart matched with counterpart,\" and `خ ل ق` B002, originating and bringing into being. **Cue:** the same `وَإِلَى ... كَيْفَ` frame continues through sky, mountains, and earth in 88:18-20. **Role:** the counterpart branch makes each object a comparison case; creation is distributed across organism, overhead structure, vertical mass, and horizontal surface. **Abduction:** I infer that repeated syntax authorizes cross-domain analogy rather than four unrelated observations. **Reading change:** the camel reads as an entry point into a system of correspondences about how unlike forms are each fitted to a role.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B001","root_000006/B002","root_000434/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c5658df7a3f7110fa5be","text":"2. **The camel is a biological answer to the water problem posed by the nearby spring.** **Lexical:** `ء ب ل` B001, camels and skilled tending, and B002, \"sufficing without water\" by fresh herbage; fixed `خ ل ق` B003 supplies formed fitness. **Cue:** the focus window begins with a running spring in 88:12, then moves through a supplied habitat before selecting the camel in 88:17. **Role:** the spring models continuous external provision; the camel branch models bodily resilience that reduces immediate dependence on such provision. **Abduction:** I infer that the juxtaposition invites a comparison of two life-support strategies. **Reading change:** the camel's creation becomes functionally specific: its visible form can be inspected as an adaptation to uneven water access.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_001520/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dd11a363081d1eb44b27","text":"1. **Looking is a method for recovering hidden proportion from visible form.** **Lexical:** `ن ظ ر` B001, directing sight or insight to apprehend a thing; `خ ل ق` B001, measuring and proportioning; and B003, complete, balanced visible form. No accepted branch inventory is supplied for `ك ي ف`, so its repeated construction serves only as a structural cue. **Cue:** `يَنظُرُونَ` governs a visible animal, while passive `خُلِقَتْ` with `كَيْفَ` asks from present form back toward its making. **Role:** sight collects the evidence, proportion supplies the hidden design operation, and completed form is the observable result. **Abduction:** I infer measured design from the branch and the how-question; the ayah itself leaves the manner unstated. **Reading change:** the command is not a request merely to notice a camel but to reverse-engineer proportion from embodied evidence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B001","root_000006/B002","root_000434/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_df5f783002ce3794fb3d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:17:4\",\"root_000006\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"88:17:6\",\"root_000434\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Devenin bedensel biçimi, yakın çevredeki su sorununa farklı bir çözüm olarak düzensiz su erişimine dayanacak şekilde uyarlanmış görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e4f36038527453d46324","text":"3. **Creation as measured proportion, not fabricated report.** Evidence: `خ ل ق` B001 measuring/proportioning, B007 fabricating false speech; `ح د ث` B003 report in 88:1; `ك ف ر` B003 covering truth later. Structural cue: true report and visible creation frame the later refusal. Role: `خلق` supplies real measured form against invented or rejected accounts. Abductive move: looking at creation exposes the difference between formed reality and covered/fabricated speech. Reading change: `كيف خلقت` asks about intelligible proportion and truth, not merely existence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fed8afac18bc89265398","text":"2. **Camel as key to the earlier deprivation chain.** Evidence: `ء ب ل` B002 sufficing without water; earlier `س ق ي`, `ض ر ع`, `س م ن`, `ج و ع`; `خ ش ع` B004 depleted hump. Structural cue: the first object of inspection is the camel after a punishment section about drink, pasture, fatness, and hunger. Role: the camel gathers the water-fat-hump-pasture system. Abductive move: the surah chooses a creature whose body visibly solves the problems punishment intensifies. Reading change: the question is not generic natural theology; it retroactively interprets the food/water imagery.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
